953	
954	// Keyword extraction: clickable stem + translation textarea + reveal.
955	function KeywordExtractionRenderer({
956	  item,
957	  onAnswer,
958	  onNext,
959	}: {
960	  item: typeof TRANSLATOR_ITEMS[number];
961	  onAnswer: (verdict: boolean | QuizVerdict) => void;
962	  onNext: () => void;
963	}) {
964	  const { markTranslatorComplete } = useApp();
965	  const tokens = useMemo(() => splitTokens(item.stemText), [item.stemText]);
966	  const [picked, setPicked] = useState<Set<number>>(new Set());
967	  const [translation, setTranslation] = useState("");
968	  const [submitted, setSubmitted] = useState(false);
969	
970	  const correctLower = item.correctKeywords.map((k) => k.toLowerCase());
971	
972	  const togglePick = (i: number) => {
973	    if (submitted) return;
974	    setPicked((prev) => {
975	      const next = new Set(prev);
976	      if (next.has(i)) next.delete(i);
977	      else next.add(i);
978	      return next;
979	    });
980	  };
981	
982	  const canSubmit = picked.size > 0 && translation.trim().length >= 10;
983	
984	  const pickedWords = Array.from(picked).map((i) => stripPunc(tokens[i]).toLowerCase());
985	  const matched = pickedWords.filter((w) => correctLower.includes(w));
986	  const wrong = pickedWords.filter((w) => !correctLower.includes(w));
987	  const pctCorrect = correctLower.length > 0 ? matched.length / correctLower.length : 0;
988	  const verdict: QuizVerdict =
989	    pctCorrect >= 0.75 && wrong.length === 0
990	      ? "correct"
991	      : pctCorrect >= 0.75 && wrong.length <= 2
992	        ? "partial"
993	        : "incorrect";
994	
995	  const submit = () => {
996	    setSubmitted(true);
997	    onAnswer(verdict);
998	    void markTranslatorComplete(
999	      item.stemNumber,
1000	      Array.from(picked).map((i) => tokens[i]),
1001	      translation,
1002	    );
1003	  };
1004	
1005	  return (
1006	    <div className="bg-brutal-paper border-4 border-brutal-ink p-6 brutal-shadow">
1007	      <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest mb-2">Stem {item.stemNumber} of {TRANSLATOR_ITEMS.length}</p>
1008	      <div className="bg-brutal-paper border-4 border-brutal-ink p-5 mb-4">
1009	        <p className="text-xl italic font-black leading-snug">
1010	          {tokens.map((t, i) => {
1011	            const isPicked = picked.has(i);
1012	            const isWord = /\w/.test(t);
1013	            const isCorrectWord = submitted && correctLower.includes(stripPunc(t).toLowerCase());
1014	            const isWrongPick = submitted && isPicked && !isCorrectWord;
1015	            const cls = cn(
1016	              "transition-colors",
1017	              isWord ? "cursor-pointer" : "",
1018	              !submitted && isPicked ? "bg-brutal-yellow" : "",
1019	              submitted && isCorrectWord && isPicked ? "bg-brutal-green" : "",
1020	              submitted && isCorrectWord && !isPicked ? "bg-brutal-yellow" : "",
1021	              isWrongPick ? "bg-brutal-red text-brutal-paper" : "",
1022	            );
1023	            return (
1024	              <span
1025	                key={i}
1026	                onClick={() => isWord && togglePick(i)}
1027	                className={cls}
1028	              >
1029	                {t}
1030	              </span>
1031	            );
1032	          })}
1033	        </p>
1034	      </div>
1035	      <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest mb-2">Step 2: Plain English</p>
1036	      <textarea
1037	        value={translation}
1038	        onChange={(e) => setTranslation(e.target.value)}
1039	        disabled={submitted}
1040	        placeholder="Translate the stem in your own words..."
1041	        className="w-full min-h-[100px] border-2 border-brutal-ink p-3 font-bold bg-white"
1042	      />
1043	      {!submitted && (
1044	        <button
1045	          type="button"
1046	          disabled={!canSubmit}
1047	          onClick={submit}
1048	          className={cn(
1049	            "mt-4 brutal-btn",
1050	            canSubmit ? "bg-brutal-yellow" : "bg-brutal-paper opacity-60",
1051	          )}
1052	        >
1053	          Submit
1054	        </button>
1055	      )}
1056	      {submitted && (
1057	        <div className="mt-5 space-y-4">
1058	          <VerdictHeader verdict={verdict} />
1059	          <p className="text-sm font-bold">
1060	            You picked {matched.length} correct keyword{matched.length === 1 ? "" : "s"} out of {correctLower.length}.{" "}
1061	            {wrong.length > 0
1062	              ? `You also picked ${wrong.length} word${wrong.length === 1 ? "" : "s"} that ${wrong.length === 1 ? "is" : "are"} not load-bearing.`
1063	              : "No off-target picks."}
1064	          </p>
1065	          <div className="bg-brutal-cyan/30 border-2 border-brutal-ink p-4">
1066	            <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest mb-2">Ideal keywords</p>
1067	            <p className="font-bold">
1068	              {item.correctKeywords.map((k, i) => (
1069	                <span
1070	                  key={i}
1071	                  className="inline-block bg-brutal-green border-2 border-brutal-ink px-2 py-0.5 mr-2 mb-1"
1072	                >
1073	                  {k}
1074	                </span>
1075	              ))}
1076	            </p>
1077	          </div>
1078	          <div className="bg-brutal-cyan/30 border-2 border-brutal-ink p-4">
1079	            <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest mb-2">Ideal translation</p>
1080	            <p className="font-bold italic">{item.idealTranslation}</p>
1081	          </div>
1082	          <div className="bg-white border-2 border-brutal-ink p-4">
1083	            <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest mb-2">Walkthrough</p>
1084	            {item.walkthrough.reasoning.map((p, i) => (
1085	              <p key={i} className="font-bold mb-2"><TermText>{p}</TermText></p>
1086	            ))}
1087	          </div>
1088	          {item.walkthrough.vocab.length > 0 && (
1089	            <div className="space-y-2">
1090	              {item.walkthrough.vocab.map((v, i) => (
1091	                <div key={i} className="bg-brutal-yellow border-2 border-brutal-ink p-3">
1092	                  <p className="font-black uppercase text-xs tracking-widest">{v.term}</p>
1093	                  <p className="font-bold">{v.meaning}</p>
1094	                  {v.note && <p className="text-xs italic mt-1">{v.note}</p>}
1095	                </div>
1096	              ))}
1097	            </div>
1098	          )}
1099	          {item.walkthrough.requirements.length > 0 && (
1100	            <div className="bg-brutal-cyan border-2 border-brutal-ink p-4">
1101	              <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest mb-2">Stimulus must have</p>
1102	              <ul className="list-disc pl-5 space-y-1">
1103	                {item.walkthrough.requirements.map((r, i) => (
1104	                  <li key={i} className="font-bold">{r}</li>
1105	                ))}
1106	              </ul>
1107	            </div>
1108	          )}
1109	          <div className="bg-white border-2 border-brutal-ink p-4">
1110	            <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest mb-2">Worked example</p>
1111	            <p className="italic font-bold mb-2">{item.walkthrough.example.stimulus}</p>
1112	            <p className="font-bold text-brutal-ink-soft">{item.walkthrough.example.explanation}</p>
1113	          </div>
1114	          <button
1115	            type="button"
1116	            onClick={onNext}
1117	            className="brutal-btn bg-brutal-green inline-flex items-center gap-2"
1118	          >
1119	            Next stem <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4" />
1120	          </button>
1121	        </div>
1122	      )}
1123	    </div>
1124	  );
1125	}
1126	
1127	function VerdictHeader({ verdict }: { verdict: QuizVerdict }) {
1128	  const tone =
1129	    verdict === "correct"
1130	      ? "bg-brutal-green"
1131	      : verdict === "partial"
1132	        ? "bg-brutal-yellow"
1133	        : "bg-brutal-red text-brutal-paper";
1134	  const label =
1135	    verdict === "correct" ? "Correct" : verdict === "partial" ? "Partial" : "Try again";
1136	  return (
1137	    <div
1138	      className={cn(
1139	        "inline-block border-4 border-brutal-ink px-4 py-2 text-sm font-black uppercase tracking-wider",
1140	        tone,
1141	      )}
1142	    >
1143	      {label}
1144	    </div>
1145	  );
1146	}
1147	
1148	function splitTokens(text: string): string[] {
1149	  return text.split(/(\s+|[.,;:?!"()])/).filter((t) => t.length > 0);
1150	}
1151	
1152	function stripPunc(s: string): string {
1153	  return s.replace(/[.,;:?!"()]/g, "");
1154	}
1155	
1156	// Match-to-stimulus: read stimulus + answer choice, judge each part.
1157	function MatchToStimulusRenderer({
1158	  item,
1159	  onAnswer,
1160	  onNext,
1161	}: {
1162	  item: typeof MATCHING_ITEMS[number];
1163	  onAnswer: (verdict: boolean | QuizVerdict) => void;
1164	  onNext: () => void;
1165	}) {
1166	  const [partIndex, setPartIndex] = useState(0);
1167	  const [decisions, setDecisions] = useState<("match" | "no_match")[]>([]);
1168	  const [revealed, setRevealed] = useState(false);
1169	
1170	  const currentPart = item.parts[partIndex];
1171	
1172	  const decide = (choice: "match" | "no_match") => {
1173	    const next = [...decisions, choice];
1174	    setDecisions(next);
1175	    if (partIndex + 1 < item.parts.length) {
1176	      setPartIndex(partIndex + 1);
1177	    } else {
1178	      const allCorrect = next.every((d, i) =>
1179	        d === "match" ? item.parts[i].matches : !item.parts[i].matches,
1180	      );
1181	      onAnswer(allCorrect);
1182	      setRevealed(true);
1183	    }
1184	  };
1185	
1186	  return (
1187	    <div className="bg-brutal-paper border-4 border-brutal-ink p-6 brutal-shadow">
1188	      <div className="bg-white border-2 border-brutal-ink p-4 mb-4">
1189	        <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest mb-2">Stimulus</p>
1190	        <p className="font-bold whitespace-pre-line">{item.stimulus}</p>
1191	      </div>
1192	      <div className="bg-brutal-yellow border-2 border-brutal-ink p-4 mb-5">
1193	        <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest mb-2">Answer choice</p>
1194	        <p className="font-bold italic">{item.answerChoice}</p>
1195	      </div>
1196	      {!revealed ? (
1197	        <>
1198	          <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest mb-2">
1199	            Part {partIndex + 1} of {item.parts.length}
1200	          </p>
1201	          <div className="bg-white border-2 border-brutal-ink p-4 mb-4">
1202	            <p className="font-bold">{currentPart.text}</p>
1203	          </div>
1204	          <div className="flex gap-3">
1205	            <button
1206	              type="button"
1207	              onClick={() => decide("match")}
1208	              className="brutal-btn bg-brutal-green flex-1"
1209	            >
1210	              Match
1211	            </button>
1212	            <button
1213	              type="button"
1214	              onClick={() => decide("no_match")}
1215	              className="brutal-btn bg-brutal-red text-brutal-paper flex-1"
1216	            >
1217	              No match
1218	            </button>
1219	          </div>
1220	        </>
1221	      ) : (
1222	        <div className="space-y-3">
1223	          {item.parts.map((p, i) => {
1224	            const correct = decisions[i] === "match" ? p.matches : !p.matches;
1225	            return (
1226	              <div
1227	                key={i}
1228	                className={cn(
1229	                  "border-2 border-brutal-ink p-3",
1230	                  correct ? "bg-brutal-green" : "bg-brutal-red/30",
1231	                )}
1232	              >
1233	                <p className="font-bold mb-1">{p.text}</p>
1234	                <p className="text-xs italic">{p.explanation}</p>
1235	              </div>
1236	            );
1237	          })}
1238	          <button
1239	            type="button"
1240	            onClick={onNext}
1241	            className="brutal-btn bg-brutal-yellow inline-flex items-center gap-2"
1242	          >
1243	            Next <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4" />
1244	          </button>
1245	        </div>
1246	      )}
1247	    </div>
1248	  );
1249	}
1250	
1251	// Decoding Gauntlet: 3-step inline (keyword + translation + requirements checklist).
1252	function DecodingGauntletRenderer({
1253	  item,
1254	  onAnswer,
1255	  onNext,
1256	}: {
1257	  item: typeof ABSTRACT_CHOICES[number];
1258	  onAnswer: (verdict: boolean | QuizVerdict) => void;
1259	  onNext: () => void;
1260	}) {
1261	  const { recordGauntletItem } = useApp();
1262	  const tokens = useMemo(() => splitTokens(item.original), [item.original]);
1263	  const correctLower = item.correctKeywords.map((k) => k.toLowerCase());
1264	  const [picked, setPicked] = useState<Set<number>>(new Set());
1265	  const [translation, setTranslation] = useState("");
1266	  const [reqChecked, setReqChecked] = useState<Set<number>>(new Set());
1267	  const [submitted, setSubmitted] = useState(false);
1268	
1269	  const pickedWords = Array.from(picked).map((i) => stripPunc(tokens[i]).toLowerCase());
1270	  const matched = pickedWords.filter((w) =>
1271	    correctLower.some((k) => k.split(/\s+/).includes(w)),
1272	  );
1273	  const wrong = pickedWords.filter(
1274	    (w) => !correctLower.some((k) => k.split(/\s+/).includes(w)),
1275	  );
1276	  // Count distinct correct keyword phrases hit (one count per ideal phrase).
1277	  const phrasesHit = correctLower.filter((k) =>
1278	    k.split(/\s+/).some((part) => pickedWords.includes(part)),
1279	  ).length;
1280	  const pctCorrect = correctLower.length > 0 ? phrasesHit / correctLower.length : 0;
1281	  const verdict: QuizVerdict =
1282	    pctCorrect >= 0.75 && wrong.length === 0
1283	      ? "correct"
1284	      : pctCorrect >= 0.75 && wrong.length <= 2
1285	        ? "partial"
1286	        : "incorrect";
1287	
1288	  const submit = () => {
1289	    setSubmitted(true);
1290	    onAnswer(verdict);
1291	    void recordGauntletItem({
1292	      choiceId: item.id,
1293	      keywordsPicked: Array.from(picked).map((i) => tokens[i]),
1294	      translationText: translation,
1295	      requirementsChecked: Array.from(reqChecked),
1296	      correctCount: matched.length,
1297	    });
1298	  };
1299	
1300	  return (
1301	    <div className="bg-brutal-paper border-4 border-brutal-ink p-6 brutal-shadow">
1302	      <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest mb-2">Item {item.id} of {ABSTRACT_CHOICES.length}</p>
1303	      <div className="bg-brutal-paper border-4 border-brutal-ink p-5 mb-4">
1304	        <p className="text-lg italic font-black leading-snug">
1305	          {tokens.map((t, i) => {
1306	            const isWord = /\w/.test(t);
1307	            const isPicked = picked.has(i);
1308	            const isCorrectWord = submitted && correctLower.some((k) => k.split(/\s+/).includes(stripPunc(t).toLowerCase()));
1309	            const cls = cn(
1310	              "transition-colors",
1311	              isWord ? "cursor-pointer" : "",
1312	              !submitted && isPicked ? "bg-brutal-yellow" : "",
1313	              submitted && isCorrectWord ? "bg-brutal-green" : "",
1314	              submitted && isPicked && !isCorrectWord ? "bg-brutal-red text-brutal-paper" : "",
1315	            );
1316	            return (
1317	              <span
1318	                key={i}
1319	                onClick={() => isWord && !submitted && setPicked((p) => {
1320	                  const next = new Set(p);
1321	                  if (next.has(i)) next.delete(i);
1322	                  else next.add(i);
1323	                  return next;
1324	                })}
1325	                className={cls}
1326	              >
1327	                {t}
1328	              </span>
1329	            );
1330	          })}
1331	        </p>
1332	      </div>
1333	      <textarea
1334	        value={translation}
1335	        onChange={(e) => setTranslation(e.target.value)}
1336	        disabled={submitted}
1337	        placeholder="Plain English..."
1338	        className="w-full min-h-[80px] border-2 border-brutal-ink p-3 font-bold bg-white mb-4"
1339	      />
1340	      {!submitted ? (
1341	        <button
1342	          type="button"
1343	          disabled={picked.size === 0 || translation.trim().length < 10}
1344	          onClick={submit}
1345	          className="brutal-btn bg-brutal-yellow"
1346	        >
1347	          Submit

1400	// ============ Drill ============
1401	
1402	function ModuleDrill({ moduleId, onDone }: { moduleId: ModuleId; onDone: () => void }) {
1403	  const { markDrillComplete, markModuleDrillComplete, addXp } = useApp();
1404	  const mod = getModule(moduleId)!;
1405	  const drills = useMemo(
1406	    () => mod.drillIds.map((id) => DRILLS.find((d) => d.id === id)).filter(Boolean) as DrillItem[],
1407	    [mod.drillIds],
1408	  );
1409	  const [index, setIndex] = useState(0);
1410	  const [picked, setPicked] = useState<number | null>(null);
1411	  const [correctCount, setCorrectCount] = useState(0);
1412	  const startedRef = useState(() => Date.now())[0];
1413	
1414	  if (drills.length === 0) {
1415	    return (
1416	      <div className="text-center">
1417	        <p className="font-bold mb-4">No drills configured for this module.</p>
1418	        <button onClick={onDone} className="brutal-btn bg-brutal-green">Continue</button>
1419	      </div>
1420	    );
1421	  }
1422	
1423	  const current = drills[index];
1424	
1425	  const submit = (i: number) => {
1426	    setPicked(i);
1427	    const correct = i === current.correctOptionIndex;
1428	    if (correct) {
1429	      setCorrectCount((c) => c + 1);
1430	      addXp(5);
1431	    }
1432	    void markDrillComplete(
1433	      current.id,
1434	      current.type as DrillType,
1435	      correct,
1436	      i,
1437	      "Pretty sure",
1438	      current.trapTag,
1439	      Date.now() - startedRef,
1440	    );
1441	  };
1442	
1443	  const nextItem = async () => {
1444	    if (index + 1 >= drills.length) {
1445	      await markModuleDrillComplete(moduleId);
1446	      onDone();
1447	    } else {
1448	      setPicked(null);
1449	      setIndex(index + 1);
1450	    }
1451	  };
1452	
1453	  return (
1454	    <div>
1455	      <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest mb-3 text-brutal-ink-soft">
1456	        Drill {index + 1} of {drills.length}
1457	      </p>
1458	      <div className="bg-brutal-paper border-4 border-brutal-ink p-6 brutal-shadow">
1459	        {current.stimulus && (
1460	          <div className="bg-white border-2 border-brutal-ink p-4 mb-4">
1461	            <p className="font-bold whitespace-pre-line">{current.stimulus}</p>
1462	          </div>
1463	        )}
1464	        {current.question && (
1465	          <p className="font-black mb-4">{current.question}</p>
1466	        )}
1467	        <ul className="space-y-3 list-none m-0 p-0">
1468	          {current.options.map((opt, i) => {
1469	            const isCorrect = i === current.correctOptionIndex;
1470	            const isPicked = picked === i;
1471	            const tone =
1472	              picked === null
1473	                ? "bg-white hover:bg-brutal-yellow/30"
1474	                : isCorrect
1475	                  ? "bg-brutal-green"
1476	                  : isPicked
1477	                    ? "bg-brutal-red text-brutal-paper"
1478	                    : "bg-white opacity-60";
1479	            return (
1480	              <li key={i}>
1481	                <button
1482	                  type="button"
1483	                  disabled={picked !== null}
1484	                  onClick={() => submit(i)}
1485	                  className={cn("w-full text-left p-4 border-2 border-brutal-ink font-bold", tone)}
1486	                >
1487	                  {opt.text}
1488	                </button>
1489	                {picked !== null && (isCorrect || isPicked) && (
1490	                  <p className="text-xs italic font-bold p-2 text-brutal-ink-soft">{opt.explanation}</p>
1491	                )}
1492	              </li>
1493	            );
1494	          })}
1495	        </ul>
1496	        {picked !== null && (
1497	          <div className="mt-5 bg-brutal-cyan/30 border-2 border-brutal-ink p-4">
1498	            <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest mb-2">Why</p>
1499	            <p className="font-bold">{current.explanation}</p>
1500	            <button
1501	              type="button"
1502	              onClick={nextItem}
1503	              className="mt-4 brutal-btn bg-brutal-yellow inline-flex items-center gap-2"
1504	            >
1505	              {index + 1 >= drills.length ? "Finish module" : "Next drill"}
1506	              <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4" />
1507	            </button>
1508	          </div>
1509	        )}
1510	      </div>
1511	    </div>
1512	  );
1513	}

1517	function ModuleComplete({ moduleId, onContinue }: { moduleId: ModuleId; onContinue: () => void }) {
1518	  const { bootcampState, openModule } = useApp();
1519	  const mod = getModule(moduleId)!;
1520	  const row = bootcampState.moduleProgress[moduleId];
1521	  const aced = bootcampState.aceBadges.includes(moduleId);
1522	  const mastery: MasteryLevel = row?.masteryLevel ?? computeMastery(row);
1523	  const nextModule = MODULES[MODULES.findIndex((m) => m.id === moduleId) + 1];
1524	  const prefersReducedMotion = useReducedMotion();
1525	
1526	  useEffect(() => {
1527	    if (prefersReducedMotion) return;
1528	    const timeout = setTimeout(() => {
1529	      confetti({
1530	        particleCount: 90,
1531	        spread: 70,
1532	        startVelocity: 35,
1533	        origin: { y: 0.3 },
1534	        ticks: 200,
1535	      });
1536	    }, 50);
1537	    return () => clearTimeout(timeout);
1538	  }, [prefersReducedMotion]);
1539	
1540	  return (
1541	    <div className="text-center py-8">
1542	      {prefersReducedMotion ? (
1543	        <div className="inline-block bg-brutal-yellow border-4 border-brutal-ink px-6 py-3 mb-4 font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-sm">
1544	          Done
1545	        </div>
1546	      ) : (
1547	        <motion.div
1548	          aria-hidden="true"
1549	          initial={{ scale: 0.5, opacity: 0 }}
1550	          animate={{ scale: 1, opacity: 1 }}
1551	          transition={{ duration: 0.5, ease: [0.22, 1, 0.36, 1] }}
1552	          className="mb-4"
1553	        >
1554	          <Trophy className="w-20 h-20 mx-auto" />
1555	        </motion.div>
1556	      )}
1557	      <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-brutal-ink-soft">
1558	        Module {mod.number}
1559	      </p>
1560	      <h2 className="text-4xl font-black uppercase tracking-tight mb-2">Done.</h2>
1561	      <MasteryBadge level={mastery} />
1562	      {aced && (
1563	        <p className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 font-black uppercase tracking-wider text-sm bg-brutal-yellow border-2 border-brutal-ink px-4 py-2 mt-3">
1564	          <Star className="w-4 h-4" /> Aced It
1565	        </p>
1566	      )}
1567	
1568	      <div className="mt-6 inline-flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 justify-center">
1569	        {row && row.quizTotal > 0 && (
1570	          <span className="bg-brutal-paper border-2 border-brutal-ink px-3 py-2 text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wider">
1571	            Quiz {row.quizScore} / {row.quizTotal}
1572	          </span>
1573	        )}
1574	        {row && row.drillTotal && (
1575	          <span className="bg-brutal-paper border-2 border-brutal-ink px-3 py-2 text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wider">
1576	            Drill {row.drillCorrect ?? 0} / {row.drillTotal}
1577	          </span>
1578	        )}
1579	      </div>
1580	
1581	      <p className="font-bold mt-6 mb-6">Nice. Next.</p>
1582	      {nextModule ? (
1583	        <div>
1584	          <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-brutal-ink-soft mb-3">
1585	            Next module: Module {nextModule.number}: {nextModule.name}
1586	          </p>
1587	          <button
1588	            type="button"
1589	            onClick={() => openModule(nextModule.id)}
1590	            className="brutal-btn bg-brutal-yellow inline-flex items-center gap-2"
1591	          >
1592	            Continue <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4" />
1593	          </button>
1594	        </div>
1595	      ) : (
1596	        <button
1597	          type="button"
1598	          onClick={onContinue}
1599	          className="brutal-btn bg-brutal-yellow inline-flex items-center gap-2"
1600	        >
1601	          Back to Dashboard <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4" />
1602	        </button>
1603	      )}
1604	    </div>
1605	  );
1606	}

1608	export function MasteryBadge({ level }: { level: MasteryLevel }) {
1609	  if (level === "none") return null;
1610	  const tone =
1611	    level === "mastered"
1612	      ? "bg-brutal-yellow"
1613	      : level === "solid"
1614	        ? "bg-brutal-cyan"
1615	        : "bg-brutal-paper";
1616	  const label =
1617	    level === "mastered" ? "Mastered" : level === "solid" ? "Solid" : "Practicing";
1618	  return (
1619	    <span
1620	      className={cn(
1621	        "inline-flex items-center gap-2 border-2 border-brutal-ink px-3 py-1 text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest",
1622	        tone,
1623	      )}
1624	    >
1625	      <Medal className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> {label}
1626	    </span>
1627	  );
1628	}

import LessonReader from "./LessonReader";

// LessonBody: shared lesson body renderer for ModuleNote.
//
// Strips the standalone reader chrome (nav, hero title, outer card border,
// completion CTA) and applies the cased-body CSS scope so prose reads in
// regular weight at 1.7 line-height instead of the standalone view's
// uppercase italic. The hosting ModuleNote owns the scrollable container,
// the read-this button, and the quiz CTA.

export default function LessonBody({ lessonId }: { lessonId: string }) {
  return (
    <LessonReader
      lockedLessonId={lessonId}
      hideNav
      hideComplete
      hideTitle
      hideCard
      casedBody
    />
  );
}

604	                      className="p-3 bg-white border-4 border-brutal-ink hover:bg-brutal-yellow text-brutal-ink transition-all brutal-shadow hover:translate-x-[2px] hover:translate-y-[2px] hover:shadow-none"
605	                    >
606	                      <ChevronLeft className="w-8 h-8" />
607	                    </button>
608	                  </div>
609	
610	                  <div className="absolute top-1/2 -right-12 -translate-y-1/2 hidden md:block">
611	                    <button 
612	                      onClick={handleNextKeyword}
613	                      className="p-3 bg-white border-4 border-brutal-ink hover:bg-brutal-yellow text-brutal-ink transition-all brutal-shadow hover:translate-x-[2px] hover:translate-y-[2px] hover:shadow-none"
614	                    >
615	                      <ChevronRight className="w-8 h-8" />
616	                    </button>
617	                  </div>
618	
619	                  <AnimatePresence mode="wait">
620	                    <motion.div 
621	                      key={activeKeyword}
622	                      initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.98 }}
623	                      animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1 }}
624	                      exit={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.98 }}
625	                      transition={{ duration: 0.2 }}
626	                      className={cn(
627	                        "p-12 border-4 bg-white border-brutal-ink brutal-shadow-lg min-h-[450px] flex flex-col justify-between"
628	                      )}
629	                    >
630	                      <div>
631	                        <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-12">
632	                          <h5 className="font-black text-6xl tracking-tighter text-brutal-ink uppercase italic leading-none">{decodeData[activeKeyword].term}</h5>
633	                          <motion.span 
634	                            initial={{ opacity: 0, x: 10 }}
635	                            animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }}
636	                            className="text-xs font-black bg-brutal-ink text-white px-4 py-1.5 rotate-2 uppercase tracking-widest"
637	                          >
638	                            Step {activeIndex + 1}
639	                          </motion.span>
640	                        </div>
641	                        
642	                        <div className="space-y-12">
643	                          <div className="bg-brutal-green/20 border-4 border-brutal-ink p-6">
644	                            <span className="text-[11px] font-black uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-brutal-ink-soft mb-2 block">LSAT Meaning</span>
645	                            <p className="text-[32px] text-brutal-ink font-black leading-[1.1] uppercase tracking-tighter italic">{decodeData[activeKeyword].meaning}</p>
646	                          </div>
647	
648	                          <div className="grid md:grid-cols-2 gap-10">
649	                            <div className="bg-white p-6 border-4 border-brutal-ink brutal-shadow-sm rotate-[-1deg]">
650	                              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">
651	                                <MessageSquare className="w-4 h-4 text-brutal-red" />
652	                                <span className="text-brutal-ink opacity-40 uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-[10px] font-black">Coach Note</span>
653	                              </div>
654	                              <p className="text-[17px] text-brutal-ink font-bold leading-tight">{decodeData[activeKeyword].coach}</p>
655	                            </div>
656	
657	                            <div className="bg-white p-6 border-4 border-brutal-ink brutal-shadow-sm rotate-[1deg]">
658	                              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">
659	                                <Search className="w-4 h-4 text-brutal-cyan" />
660	                                <span className="text-brutal-ink opacity-40 uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-[10px] font-black">Stimulus Check</span>
661	                              </div>
662	                              <p className="text-[17px] text-brutal-ink font-bold leading-tight">{decodeData[activeKeyword].check}</p>
663	                            </div>
664	                          </div>
665	                        </div>
666	                      </div>
667	
668	                      <div className="mt-14 flex justify-center gap-4">
669	                        {keywordKeys.map((key, i) => (
670	                          <button
671	                            key={key}
672	                            onClick={() => setActiveKeyword(key)}
673	                            className={cn(
674	                              "w-4 h-4 border-2 border-brutal-ink transition-all duration-300",
675	                              activeIndex === i ? "w-12 bg-brutal-ink" : "bg-white hover:bg-brutal-ink/10"
676	                            )}
677	                          />
678	                        ))}
679	                      </div>
680	                    </motion.div>
681	                  </AnimatePresence>
682	                </div>
683	              </div>
684	
685	                  <div className="flex md:hidden justify-between mt-10">
686	                    <button 
687	                      onClick={handlePrevKeyword}
688	                      className="px-8 py-4 bg-white border-4 border-brutal-ink text-brutal-ink font-black uppercase brutal-shadow"
689	                    >
690	                      PREV
691	                    </button>
692	                    <button 
693	                      onClick={handleNextKeyword}
694	                      className="px-8 py-4 bg-brutal-ink text-white border-4 border-brutal-ink font-black uppercase brutal-shadow"
695	                    >
696	                      NEXT
697	                    </button>
698	                  </div>
699	
700	              {/* STEP 3 */}
701	              <div className="space-y-10">
702	                <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
703	                  <span className="text-xs font-black text-brutal-ink opacity-30 uppercase tracking-[0.2em]">STEP 3</span>
704	                  <h4 className="text-brutal-ink font-black text-4xl uppercase tracking-tighter">Put the pieces together</h4>
705	                  <p className="text-brutal-ink opacity-70">Now combine the three translations into one plain-English sentence.</p>
706	                </div>
707	                
708	                <div className="bg-brutal-green/20 border-4 border-brutal-ink p-12 brutal-shadow relative overflow-hidden group">
709	                  <div className="absolute top-0 right-0 p-6 opacity-5 rotate-12 group-hover:opacity-10 transition-opacity">
710	                    <CheckCircle2 className="w-24 h-24 text-brutal-ink" />
711	                  </div>
712	                  <h5 className="text-xs font-black text-brutal-ink uppercase tracking-widest mb-6 inline-block bg-white px-2 border-2 border-brutal-ink">Plain English Translation</h5>
713	                  <p className="text-3xl font-black text-brutal-ink leading-tight uppercase tracking-tighter italic">
714	                    “The argument is trying to weaken a potential explanation by saying the available evidence is not enough to support it.”
715	                  </p>
716	                </div>
717	              </div>
718	
719	              {/* STEP 4 */}
720	              <div className="space-y-10 pt-8">
721	                <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
722	                  <span className="text-xs font-black text-brutal-ink opacity-30 uppercase tracking-[0.2em]">STEP 4</span>
723	                  <h4 className="text-brutal-ink font-black text-4xl uppercase tracking-tighter">Check the stimulus</h4>
724	                  <p className="text-brutal-ink opacity-70">Use this checklist to verify every part of the answer choice.</p>
725	                </div>
726	                
727	                <div className="bg-white border-4 border-brutal-ink p-12 brutal-shadow-lg space-y-12">
728	                  <div className="space-y-6">
729	                    {[
730	                      "Is there a hypothesis, meaning a potential explanation or causal theory?",
731	                      "Does the argument try to weaken that hypothesis?",
732	                      "Is the attack specifically about weak or insufficient evidence?"
733	                    ].map((item, i) => (
734	                      <div key={i} className="flex items-start gap-6 group">
735	                        <div className="w-8 h-8 border-4 border-brutal-ink bg-white flex-shrink-0 mt-1 flex items-center justify-center transition-colors group-hover:bg-brutal-cyan">
736	                          {/* Checkbox placeholder */}
737	                        </div>
738	                        <p className="text-xl font-bold text-brutal-ink uppercase tracking-tight italic leading-none py-2">{item}</p>
739	                      </div>
740	                    ))}
741	                  </div>
742	
743	                  <div className="pt-10 border-t-4 border-brutal-ink flex flex-col md:flex-row gap-12">
744	                    <div className="space-y-2">
745	                      <span className="text-xs font-black text-brutal-green uppercase tracking-widest bg-brutal-ink px-2">Match</span>
746	                      <p className="text-brutal-ink font-black text-lg uppercase italic leading-none">All pieces present? The answer is <span className="bg-brutal-green border-2 border-brutal-ink px-1">alive</span>.</p>
747	                    </div>
748	                    <div className="space-y-2">
749	                      <span className="text-xs font-black text-brutal-pink uppercase tracking-widest bg-brutal-ink px-2">Mismatch</span>
750	                      <p className="text-brutal-ink font-black text-lg uppercase italic leading-none">One piece missing? The answer is <span className="bg-brutal-pink border-2 border-brutal-ink px-1 text-white">dead</span>.</p>
751	                    </div>
752	                  </div>
753	                </div>
754	              </div>
755	
756	              {/* FINAL TAKEAWAY */}
757	              <div className="bg-brutal-ink text-white p-12 border-4 border-brutal-ink brutal-shadow mt-16 relative overflow-hidden">
758	                <div className="absolute bottom-0 right-0 w-32 h-32 bg-white/5 translate-x-12 translate-y-12 rotate-45" />
759	                <span className="text-xs font-black text-brutal-yellow uppercase tracking-[0.2em] block mb-6">TAKEAWAY</span>
760	                <div className="space-y-6">
761	                  <p className="text-4xl font-black uppercase italic leading-tight tracking-tighter text-brutal-cyan">Do not pick an answer because it sounds related.</p>
762	                  <p className="text-2xl font-bold leading-snug bg-brutal-cyan/20 border-4 border-brutal-cyan p-6">
763	                    Extract the keywords. Translate them. Then make every meaningful part prove itself in the stimulus.
764	                  </p>
765	                </div>
766	              </div>
767	            </div>
768	          </div>
769	        );
770	
771	      case 'deabstractifying_method':
772	        return (
773	          <div className="space-y-8 text-brutal-ink font-bold text-[18px] leading-tight">
774	            <p className="max-w-[760px] mx-auto text-xl font-bold italic leading-snug">Since the answer choices use vague language, you have to master a skill called <span className="uppercase text-brutal-red italic tracking-tighter">de-abstractifying</span>.</p>
775	            
776	            <div className="max-w-[760px] mx-auto">
777	              <RuleCard>
778	                De-abstractifying means translating vague or abstract answer-choice language into the concrete thing it refers to in the stimulus.
779	              </RuleCard>
780	            </div>
781	
782	            <p className="max-w-[760px] mx-auto">This is a precise 3-step process:</p>
783	
784	            <div className="bg-white border-4 border-brutal-ink p-10 brutal-shadow max-w-[760px] mx-auto space-y-6 relative group">
785	              <div className="absolute top-[-20px] right-8 bg-brutal-pink border-2 border-brutal-ink px-4 py-1 font-black uppercase text-xs rotate-2 brutal-shadow-sm group-hover:rotate-0 transition-transform">REQUIRED SKILL</div>
786	              <div className="flex items-center gap-6">
787	                <div className="w-12 h-12 border-4 border-brutal-ink bg-brutal-cyan text-brutal-ink flex items-center justify-center font-black text-xl rotate-[-2deg] shrink-0 brutal-shadow-sm">1</div>
788	                <p className="text-brutal-ink font-black uppercase tracking-tighter">Extract the keywords.</p>
789	              </div>
790	              <div className="flex items-center gap-6">
791	                <div className="w-12 h-12 border-4 border-brutal-ink bg-brutal-yellow text-brutal-ink flex items-center justify-center font-black text-xl rotate-[3deg] shrink-0 brutal-shadow-sm">2</div>
792	                <p className="text-brutal-ink font-black uppercase tracking-tighter">Translate each keyword into LSAT language.</p>
793	              </div>
794	              <div className="flex items-center gap-6">
795	                <div className="w-12 h-12 border-4 border-brutal-ink bg-brutal-green text-brutal-ink flex items-center justify-center font-black text-xl rotate-[-1deg] shrink-0 brutal-shadow-sm">3</div>
796	                <p className="text-brutal-ink font-black uppercase tracking-tighter">Match each translated part back to the stimulus.</p>
797	              </div>
798	            </div>
799	            
800	            <div className="max-w-[760px] mx-auto pt-8">
801	              <Callout title="THE LSAT U WAY" variant="plain">
802	                <p className="text-2xl font-black uppercase italic tracking-tighter mb-4 text-brutal-pink leading-none">Do not ask, "Does this sound related?"</p>
803	                <p className="text-xl font-bold">Ask, "Can I point to every meaningful part of this answer choice in the stimulus?"</p>
804	              </Callout>
805	            </div>
806	            
807	            <p className="max-w-[760px] mx-auto text-brutal-ink font-black tracking-tight"><span className="bg-brutal-yellow/70 px-1">If one meaningful part does not match, the answer is dead on arrival.</span></p>
808	          </div>
809	        );
810	
811	      case 'words_to_know':
812	        return (
813	          <div className="space-y-8 text-brutal-ink font-bold text-[18px]">
814	            <p className="max-w-[760px]">To de-abstractify quickly, you need to know what certain words mean when the LSAT uses them.</p>
815	            
816	            <div className="grid md:grid-cols-2 gap-6 mt-8">
817	              {[
818	                { term: 'Presuppose', meaning: 'Require.', note: 'On the LSAT, "presuppose" means the argument requires something. It points to something necessary for the reasoning to work.' },
819	                { term: 'Illustrate', meaning: 'Example.', note: 'If an answer choice says something "illustrates" something else, the quoted statement is functioning as an example.' },
820	                { term: 'General Principle', meaning: 'Broad Rule.', note: 'A principle is not just one person’s situation. It is a broader rule about what should or should not happen.' },
821	                { term: 'Explanation', meaning: 'Cause.', note: 'On the LSAT, "explanation" usually means a cause. Look for cause-effect reasoning.' },
822	                { term: 'Hypothesis', meaning: 'Potential Explanation.', note: 'Do not flatten "hypothesis" into "claim." A hypothesis is usually a proposed explanation for WHY.' },
823	                { term: 'Premise', meaning: 'Support.', note: 'A statement offered as evidence for a conclusion.' },
824	                { term: 'Intermediate Conclusion', meaning: 'Stepping Stone.', note: 'A statement supported by others and supporting the final point.' },
825	                { term: 'Main Conclusion', meaning: 'Final Point.', note: 'The ultimate claim the author wants you to accept.' },
826	              ].map((item, i) => (
827	                <div key={i} className="bg-white p-6 border-4 border-brutal-ink brutal-shadow transition-transform hover:translate-x-[-2px] hover:translate-y-[-2px] hover:shadow-[6px_6px_0px_0px_#000]">
828	                  <span className={cn("inline-block px-2 py-0.5 border-2 border-brutal-ink text-[10px] font-black uppercase mb-3", i % 4 === 0 ? "bg-brutal-yellow" : i % 4 === 1 ? "bg-brutal-cyan" : i % 4 === 2 ? "bg-brutal-pink" : "bg-brutal-green")}>
829	                    LSAT DICTIONARY
830	                  </span>
831	                  <h5 className="text-2xl font-black text-brutal-ink uppercase mb-1">{item.term}</h5>
832	                  <p className="text-lg font-black text-brutal-ink opacity-80 mb-4 italic leading-none">{item.meaning}</p>
833	                  <p className="text-[14px] leading-tight opacity-70 font-bold">{item.note}</p>
834	                </div>
835	              ))}
836	            </div>
837	          </div>
838	        );
839	
840	      case 'abstract_15':
841	        return (
842	          <div className="space-y-12 text-brutal-ink font-bold text-[18px] leading-tight">
843	            <div className="max-w-[760px] mx-auto flex flex-col items-center text-center gap-4">
844	              <p className="opacity-50 text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-[0.2em]">BOOTCAMP DECODE CHALLENGE</p>
845	              <h2 className="text-5xl font-black uppercase italic tracking-tighter leading-none">The Decoding Gauntlet</h2>
846	              <p className="max-w-xl">You must decode all 18 abstract choices to continue. Break them apart. Translate them. Force them to prove themselves.</p>
847	            </div>
848	            
849	            <div className="max-w-[760px] mx-auto bg-white border-4 border-brutal-ink p-10 brutal-shadow relative group">
850	              <div className="absolute top-[-20px] left-8 bg-brutal-ink text-white border-2 border-brutal-ink px-4 py-1 font-black uppercase text-xs rotate-[-1deg] brutal-shadow-sm group-hover:rotate-0 transition-transform">GAUNTLET RULES</div>
851	              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-8 text-sm">
852	                <div className="flex items-start gap-4">
853	                  <div className="w-8 h-8 flex-shrink-0 border-2 border-brutal-ink bg-brutal-cyan flex items-center justify-center font-black text-xs">1</div>
854	                  <p className="font-bold uppercase tracking-tight leading-tight">Pick the keywords doing the work.</p>
855	                </div>
856	                <div className="flex items-start gap-4">
857	                  <div className="w-8 h-8 flex-shrink-0 border-2 border-brutal-ink bg-brutal-yellow flex items-center justify-center font-black text-xs">2</div>
858	                  <p className="font-bold uppercase tracking-tight leading-tight">Translate them into LSAT meanings.</p>
859	                </div>
860	                <div className="flex items-start gap-4">
861	                  <div className="w-8 h-8 flex-shrink-0 border-2 border-brutal-ink bg-brutal-pink flex items-center justify-center font-black text-xs">3</div>
862	                  <p className="font-bold uppercase tracking-tight leading-tight">Identify stimulus requirements.</p>
863	                </div>
864	                <div className="flex items-start gap-4">
865	                  <div className="w-8 h-8 flex-shrink-0 border-2 border-brutal-ink bg-brutal-green flex items-center justify-center font-black text-xs">4</div>
866	                  <p className="font-bold uppercase tracking-tight leading-tight">Check your work against coach notes.</p>
867	                </div>
868	              </div>
869	            </div>
870	
871	            <DecodeGauntlet onComplete={() => setGauntletFinished(true)} />
872	          </div>
873	        );
874	
875	      case 'decode_practice':
876	        return (
877	          <div className="space-y-12 text-brutal-ink font-bold text-[18px] leading-tight max-w-[760px] mx-auto">
878	            <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
879	              <span className="text-xs font-black text-brutal-ink opacity-30 uppercase tracking-[0.2em]">PRACTICE DRILL</span>
880	              <h2 className="text-5xl font-black uppercase italic tracking-tighter leading-none">De-abstractifying Drill</h2>
881	              <p className="opacity-70">Apply the method to a real abstract choice. No guessing. Just matching.</p>
882	            </div>
883	
884	            <div className="bg-white border-4 border-brutal-ink p-12 brutal-shadow-lg relative overflow-hidden group">
885	              <div className="absolute top-0 right-0 w-12 h-12 bg-brutal-ink flex items-center justify-center text-white font-black italic">!</div>
886	              <h3 className="text-[12px] text-brutal-ink font-black uppercase tracking-widest mb-6 bg-brutal-yellow inline-block px-2 border-2 border-brutal-ink">Stimulus Segment</h3>
887	
888	              <div className="bg-brutal-ink/5 border-4 border-brutal-ink p-8 mb-8 group-hover:bg-brutal-cyan/10 transition-colors">
889	                <p className="text-[26px] text-brutal-ink font-black uppercase tracking-tighter leading-none italic">
890	                  "It is a hypothesis that the argument attempts to undermine by calling into question the sufficiency of the evidence."
891	                </p>
892	              </div>
893	
894	              <DecodePracticeCard />
895	            </div>
896	          </div>
897	        );
898	
899	      case 'master_keys':
900	        return (
901	          <div className="space-y-10 text-brutal-ink font-bold text-[18px] leading-tight max-w-[760px] mx-auto">
902	            <Callout title="Three Master Keys">
903	              Three words appear over and over inside abstract LR answer choices. Strip them out and the choice usually decodes itself.
904	            </Callout>
905	            <RuleCard>
906	              EXPLANATION = CAUSE. HYPOTHESIS = POTENTIAL CAUSE. PRESUPPOSE = REQUIRE.
907	            </RuleCard>
908	            <MethodStep step={1} title="Explanation = cause" explanation="On the LSAT, the word explanation has one meaning: the cause that produced the effect. It does not mean clarifying or elaborating." action="Whenever you see explanation, scan the stimulus for cause-effect language. If there is no cause-effect, the choice is wrong." />
909	            <MethodStep step={2} title="Hypothesis = potential cause" explanation="A hypothesis is a posited causal explanation: WHY something is the way it is. Darwin's hypothesis was that species change over time. It is a causal guess, not a prediction." action="When you see hypothesis, look for a proposed causal story. Strengthen / Weaken questions live here." />
910	            <MethodStep step={3} title="Presuppose = require" explanation="In everyday English, presupposes can mean assumes. On the LSAT, presuppose simply means: the argument requires this to be true. Necessary condition territory." action="When you see presupposes, ask: if this were false, would the argument fall apart? If yes, you've matched the keyword." />
911	            <Callout title="The other four key terms" variant="plain">
912	              Facts, Decision, Principle, and Generalization round out the seven keyword set. They are tested less directly but appear inside argument structures (premise vs background, prescription vs description).
913	            </Callout>
914	            <Checklist items={[
915	              "Spot the master key word in the answer choice.",
916	              "Translate it into its LSAT meaning before re-reading the stimulus.",
917	              "Check whether the stimulus actually has that structure.",
918	            ]} />
919	          </div>
920	        );
921	
922	      case 'five_bucket_indicators':
923	        return (
924	          <div className="space-y-10 text-brutal-ink font-bold text-[18px] leading-tight max-w-[760px] mx-auto">
925	            <Callout title="Five Buckets, Five Indicator Sets">
926	              Every LR stimulus uses words that signal what kind of reasoning is happening. Sort each phrase you see into one of five buckets and the structural shape of the argument falls out.
927	            </Callout>
928	            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
929	              <MethodStep step={1} title="Assumption" explanation="Unstated premise. The argument needs it but does not say it." action="Indicators: presupposes, takes for granted, requires, on the assumption that, implicit premise." />
930	              <MethodStep step={2} title="Causal" explanation="A cause is doing work in the argument." action="Indicators: explanation, hypothesis, causes, results in, due to, account for, leads to, responsible for." />
931	              <MethodStep step={3} title="Comparison" explanation="Two things are being lined up against each other." action="Indicators: analogous to, similar to, parallel to, compared with, contrasted with, just as, like." />
932	              <MethodStep step={4} title="Evidence" explanation="What is being offered as support, and how strong is it." action="Indicators: evidence, grounds, supports, justifies, demonstrates, establishes, substantiates, on the basis of." />
933	              <MethodStep step={5} title="Evaluation" explanation="The author is judging or assessing something." action="Indicators: undermines, calls into question, casts doubt on, vindicates, refutes, criticizes, defends." />
934	            </div>
935	            <RuleCard>
936	              EVERY ABSTRACT ANSWER CHOICE WORD BELONGS IN ONE OF FIVE BUCKETS. SORT FIRST, MATCH SECOND.
937	            </RuleCard>
938	          </div>
939	        );
940	
941	      case 'art_of_matching':
942	        return (
943	          <div className="space-y-10 text-brutal-ink font-bold text-[18px] leading-tight max-w-[760px] mx-auto">
944	            <Callout title="The Art of Matching Keywords">
945	              For Role, Method, and Flaw questions, eliminate any answer choice that describes something that did not happen in the argument. The keywords are your lie detector.
946	            </Callout>
947	            <RuleCard>
948	              IDENTIFY (S)TRUCTURE → (K)EYWORDS. NEVER PICK AN ANSWER YOU CAN'T MATCH UP WITH THE STIMULUS.
949	            </RuleCard>
950	            <MethodStep step={1} title="Read the answer over and extract keywords" explanation="Strip down each abstract answer choice to its load-bearing words. Highlight or jot them." action="Skip filler verbs (used, is, makes). Keep nouns, action verbs, and qualifiers." />
951	            <MethodStep step={2} title="Analyze keywords in LSAT context" explanation="A word on the LSAT often means something different from its daily usage. Translate each keyword into its LSAT meaning." action="Cross-check with the seven key terms (presuppose, illustrate, general principle, explanation, hypothesis, facts, generalization)." />
952	            <MethodStep step={3} title="Check keyword relevance" explanation="For each keyword, decide what role it should play if the choice is correct." action="Noun: does the concept appear in the stimulus? Verb: does the author do this? Adjective/adverb: does it correctly describe tone or strength?" />
953	            <MethodStep step={4} title="Match each keyword back to the structure" explanation="Re-read the choice and try to align each keyword with a piece of the stimulus. # LSAT Logical Reasoning: Level 1 Answer Parsing & Mapping

**Student Name:** ________________ **Date:** ________________

## Instructions

To master Method of Reasoning and Flaw questions, you must be able to "de-stractify" the answer choices. Do not just read these phrases; map them.

- **The Conclusion** is the Destination (where the argument is going).
- **The Evidence** is the "Method of Transportation" (how it gets there).

## Part I: The Vocabulary of Logic

*Before mapping structure, you must define the terms accurately. In the context of the LSAT, define the strict logical meaning of the following terms.*

| Term | LSAT Definition & Requirement |
|---|---|
| **1. "Explanation"** | *Hint: It does not mean "elaborating" or "providing detail."* **Strict Definition:** _________________ |
| **2. "Defend"** | *Hint: Stating a motivation is not a defense.* **Strict Definition:** _________________ |
| **3. "Aesthetic Principle"** | *Hint: It is not just a specific opinion about one painting.* **Strict Definition:** _________________ |
| **4. "Supported Premise"** | *Hint: It functions as both a roof and a floor.* **Also known as:** _________________ |

## Part II: Structural Mapping (X vs. Y)

*For the following abstract answer choices, identifying the variable is not enough. You must identify the **Indicator Word** that tells you the direction of the logic.*

### Task

1. **Circle** the Indicator Word (e.g., "because," "from," "by").
2. Identify which variable represents the **Conclusion (C)** and which represents the **Evidence (E)**.

| Abstract Phrase | Indicator Word | Conclusion | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| *Example: infers that X on the grounds that Y* | *on the grounds that* | **X** | **Y** |
| 1. doing **X** by doing **Y** | | | |
| 2. using **X** to support **Y** | | | |
| 3. concludes, from the claim that **X**, that **Y** | | | |
| 4. concludes **X** on the basis of **Y** | | | |
| 5. infers, on the grounds that **X**, that **Y** | | | |
| 6. takes for granted that **Y** because **X** | | | |
| 7. **Y** is true merely on the basis of **X** | | | |

## Part III: Reverse Engineering & Context

*In this section, we move from abstract variables (X/Y) to specific content. You must "reverse engineer" the answer choice to determine what the stimulus must look like.*

**8. The Phrase:** *"Fails to consider that even if X, may not be Y"*

- **Logical Translation:** This describes a Flaw. The argument assumes that if you have **X** (Evidence), you generally get **Y** (Conclusion/Effect).
- **Write a Real-World Example:** Write a simple sentence using *raining* (X) and *getting wet* (Y) that fits this structure.
- *Your Argument:* _________________

**9. The Phrase:** *"Generalized too readily from the fruits it tested to the fruits it did not test"*

- **Reverse Engineer the Stimulus:** For this answer choice to be correct, what **two specific things** must exist in the text of the stimulus?
- *Requirement A (The Evidence):* _________________
- *Requirement B (The Conclusion):* _________________

**10. The Phrase:** *"Explaining a historical fact in terms of the artistic preferences of people"*

- **Logical Translation:** Remember, "Explanation" means Causation.
- **Identify the Cause and Effect:**
- *The Cause (The Explainer):* _________________
- *The Effect (The Result):* _________________

# Quiz 1 — TRANSLATION & CLUSTER SENTENCES

There will be a review quiz at the end of every chapter. These quizzes make sure you're absorbing the material in each chapter. If you don't know the answers to the quiz questions, there's more in the chapter for you to learn.

The quizzes are designed to be low-effort and require a basic minimum comprehension of the ideas discussed in the chapter. If you can't complete the quiz, you need to reread the chapter.

## Instructions

Answer these questions based on your knowledge of the chapter. Attempt to answer without looking back at the chapter first. If you don't know the answer, circle the question number and go find the answer in the chapter. Study the sections of the chapter that you couldn't remember at first.

## Word Bank

| | | |
|---|---|---|
| core(s) | optional element(s) | core element(s) |
| middle-out | sentence | specifier(s) |
| translation | studying | cluster sentence(s) |

Use the word bank to answer the fill-in-the-blank questions. Some words will not be used and others may be used more than once.

1. A(n) ________ contains at least one complete thought.
2. ________ are those little words that glue bigger words to explanations.
3. To tackle a cluster sentence with two or more commas, use ________.
4. ________ is the one skill you need to master Logical Reasoning.
5. **TRUE OR FALSE** — A core element is a piece of the sentence that can be removed without any bad grammatical consequences.
6. **TRUE OR FALSE** — Commas indicate that something on either side of the comma is a core element.
7. **TRUE OR FALSE** — The test writers purposefully write poorly.
8. **Seek and find!** Circle only the specifiers in the word cloud.

| | | | |
|---|---|---|---|
| after | them | who | interrogatory |
| long | since | comma | ostentatious |
| when | cluster | for | that |

# Argument Parsing Worksheet

**Name:** ____________________

**Date:** ____________________

## Instructions

For each phrase below, identify the different parts of the argument as requested.

### Part 1: Conclusion & Evidence

*The Conclusion is the main point or claim. The Evidence is the reason or justification for the conclusion.*

1. **"Infers that X on the grounds that Y"** — Conclusion: ________ . Evidence: ________ .
2. **"Doing X by doing Y"** — Conclusion (the goal): ________ . Evidence (the method): ________ .
3. **"Using X to support Y"** — Conclusion: ________ . Evidence: ________ .
4. **"Concludes, from the claim that X, that Y"** — Conclusion: ________ . Evidence: ________ .

### Part 2: Premise & Conclusion

5. **"Concludes X on the basis of Y"** — Premise: ________ . Conclusion: ________ .
6. **"Infers, on the grounds that X, that Y"** — Premise: ________ . Conclusion: ________ .
7. **"Takes for granted that Y because X"** — Premise: ________ . Conclusion: ________ .
8. **"Y is true merely on the basis of X"** — Premise: ________ . Conclusion: ________ .

### Part 3: Bonus

9. **What is another name for a "Supported Premise?"** — ________________

# Answer Key: Advanced Parsing

## Part I: The Vocabulary

*Use these definitions to calibrate your understanding of LSAT-specific terminology.*

**1. "Explanation"**

- **Strict Definition:** **Causation** (Cause & Effect).
- *Why?* On the LSAT, to "explain" something is to identify the cause that produced the effect. It does not mean simply describing a situation.

**2. "Defend"**

- **Strict Definition:** Advocating for or justifying a position as "right".
- *Why?* Merely stating a person's motivation (e.g., "they did it for money") is not a defense; a defense implies the author agrees with or supports the action.

**3. "Aesthetic Principle"**

- **Strict Definition:** A broad normative rule or "rule of thumb" applying to multiple scenarios.
- *Why?* If a rule applies only to one specific situation (e.g., "This painting is bad"), it is just a judgment. A principle must be broad enough to apply to many situations.

**4. "Supported Premise"**

- **Also known as:** **Intermediate Conclusion** (or Sub-Conclusion).
- *Why?* It functions as a conclusion because it is supported by evidence, but it also functions as a premise because it supports the main conclusion.

## Part II: Advanced Structural Mapping

*Key Strategy: Identify the directional word. Words like "From," "By," and "Basis" usually point backward to the Evidence.*

| Abstract Phrase | Breakdown & Logic Flow |
|---|---|
| **1. challenges the plausibility of X by offering Y as an alternative explanation** | **Conclusion:** X (is implausible) · **Evidence:** Y · *Note:* "By offering" introduces the evidence used to make the attack. |
| **2. substantiates the claim that X is false by demonstrating that Y is an inevitable consequence of X** | **Conclusion:** X is false · **Evidence:** Y (is a bad consequence) · *Note:* "Substantiates" means "supports." The author supports the idea that X is false using Y. |
| **3. undermines the support for X by drawing a distinction between Y and Z** | **Conclusion:** Support for X (is weak) · **Evidence:** Distinction between Y and Z · *Note:* The goal is to undermine X. The method is the distinction. |
| **4. rejects the hypothesis that X caused Y on the basis of Z** | **Conclusion:** Rejects "X caused Y" · **Evidence:** Z · *Note:* "On the basis of" is a classic premise indicator. |
| **5. derives the necessity of X from the impossibility of Y** | **Conclusion:** X (is necessary) · **Evidence:** Y (is impossible) · *Note:* "From" introduces the evidence source. |
| **6. treats the occurrence of X as corroborating evidence for Y** | **Conclusion:** Y · **Evidence:** X · *Note:* If X is "evidence for" Y, then X supports Y. |
| **7. X is presented in order to support the claim that Y** | **Conclusion:** Y · **Evidence:** X · *Note:* "In order to support" tells you that X is the support holding up Y. |

## Part III: Reverse Engineering High-Level Flaws

*This section confirms if you can translate abstract logic into concrete examples.*

**8. The Flaw:** *"Presumes, without providing justification, that a condition necessary for an outcome is also sufficient to produce that outcome."*

- **The Logic:** Confusing a **Requirement** with a **Guarantee**.
- **Correct Example:**
  - Premise (True): "To win the lottery, it is necessary to buy a ticket."
  - Flawed Conclusion: "I bought a ticket; therefore, I am guaranteed to win."

**9. The Flaw:** *"Rejects a position on the grounds that the proponents of the position have a vested interest in its acceptance."*

- **The Logic:** This is a **Source Argument** (Ad Hominem). Attacking the person's motive (money/power) instead of their facts.
- **Correct Example:**
  - Opposing View: "The mechanic says my brakes are dangerous and need replacing."
  - Flawed Conclusion: "He just wants to make money, so my brakes are probably fine."

**10. The Flaw:** *"Mistakes a temporal relationship between two events for a causal relationship."*

- **The Logic:** **Correlation/Temporal vs. Causation**. Just because Y happened *after* X, doesn't mean X *caused* Y.
- **Correct Example:**
  - Premise (Timeline): "The rooster crowed (X), and then the sun came up (Y)."
  - Flawed Conclusion: "Therefore, the rooster's crowing caused the sun to rise."

---

*Source: Google Doc `1Mdo68u7FxTgEsZiy31QPhqvgaU78ony3QDyCz46VVrw`. Categorized as **Abstract** (decoding abstract LSAT phrasings: vocabulary, structural mapping, reverse-engineering flaw descriptions into concrete examples).*

# Argument Parsing - Answer Key

## Part 1: Conclusion & Evidence

1. **"Infers that X on the grounds that Y"** — Conclusion: X (what is being inferred). Evidence: Y (the grounds for the inference).
2. **"Doing X by doing Y"** — Conclusion (the goal): X. Evidence (the method): Y.
3. **"Using X to support Y"** — Conclusion: Y (what is being supported). Evidence: X (what is used for support).
4. **"Concludes, from the claim that X, that Y"** — Conclusion: Y. Evidence: X (the claim it is based on).

## Part 2: Premise & Conclusion

5. **"Concludes X on the basis of Y"** — Premise: Y. Conclusion: X.
6. **"Infers, on the grounds that X, that Y"** — Premise: X. Conclusion: Y.
7. **"Takes for granted that Y because X"** — Premise: X (the reason). Conclusion: Y (the thing taken for granted).
8. **"Y is true merely on the basis of X"** — Premise: X. Conclusion: Y.

## Part 3: Vocabulary

9. **Another name for "Supported Premise"?** — Intermediate Conclusion (or Sub-Conclusion).

---

// 5 "Why is this hard?" items for Module 1.
//
// Items are derived from the coachNote and trapRequirements fields of
// existing abstractChoices items. The student sees a stimulus snippet and an
// abstract answer choice, and picks what makes that choice hard to evaluate.
// Distractors are drawn from real LSAT trap categories (extreme language,
// scope shift, role confusion, missing match) so wrong picks are educational.
//
// No fresh LSAT stimuli authored here: every stimulus + answer choice is
// reframed from material already in data/abstractChoices.ts.

export interface WhyHardItem {
  id: string;
  stimulus: string;
  answerChoice: string;
  question: string;
  options: { text: string; correct: boolean; explanation: string }[];
}

export const WHY_HARD_ITEMS: WhyHardItem[] = [
  {
    id: "wh_1",
    stimulus:
      "Tired commuters waste hours each year stuck in traffic. The city should fund the new express train, since cities that have invested in rail consistently see commute times fall.",
    answerChoice:
      "It is used to illustrate the general principle that the argument presupposes.",
    question: "What makes this answer choice hard to evaluate?",
    options: [
      {
        text: "Three LSAT-specific words (illustrate, general principle, presuppose) all carry meanings that differ from everyday usage.",
        correct: true,
        explanation:
          "On the LSAT, illustrate means example, general principle means a rule that applies across cases, and presuppose means require. All three meanings must hold for the answer to fit.",
      },
      {
        text: "The answer is hard because it uses too many short words.",
        correct: false,
        explanation: "Word count is not the obstacle. Specific LSAT vocabulary is.",
      },
      {
        text: "The answer is hard because the stimulus has no conclusion.",
        correct: false,
        explanation: "The stimulus has a clear conclusion: the city should fund the express train.",
      },
      {
        text: "The answer is hard because the stimulus contains numbers.",
        correct: false,
        explanation: "The difficulty is vocabulary, not quantitative content.",
      },
    ],
  },
  {
    id: "wh_2",
    stimulus:
      "Critics worry the festival will hurt local business. But each of the past five years that the festival ran, downtown shop revenue rose by double digits.",
    answerChoice:
      "It is used to counter a consideration that might be taken to undermine the argument's conclusion.",
    question: "What makes this answer choice hard to evaluate?",
    options: [
      {
        text: "The choice describes a structural move (preempting an objection) rather than a content claim, so the student must read the stimulus for an objection and a counter to that objection.",
        correct: true,
        explanation:
          "This is a structural role description. The stimulus must actually contain an objection (the critics' worry) and a counter to it (the revenue data).",
      },
      {
        text: "The choice is hard because it uses the word \"the\" too often.",
        correct: false,
        explanation: "Common words are not the difficulty. Structural vocabulary is.",
      },
      {
        text: "The choice is hard because it includes a number.",
        correct: false,
        explanation: "No numbers appear in the answer choice itself.",
      },
      {
        text: "The choice is hard because the stimulus contains a name.",
        correct: false,
        explanation: "Names in the stimulus do not affect the structural reading.",
      },
======
// 10 vocab quiz items for Module 2.
//
// Items 1-7 are the 7 entries from data/terms.ts (forward direction: see the
// term, pick the LSAT meaning). Items 8-10 reverse direction: see the LSAT
// meaning, pick the term. All content reframed from existing TERMS; no new
// LSAT content authored.

import { TERMS } from "./terms";

export interface TermQuizItem {
  id: string;
  prompt: string;
  options: string[];
  correctOption: number;
  explanation: string;
}

const FORWARD: TermQuizItem[] = TERMS.map((t) => ({
  id: `tq_${t.id}`,
  prompt: `On the LSAT, what does "${t.term}" mean?`,
  options: t.options,
  correctOption: t.correctOption,
  explanation: t.explanation,
}));

// Reverse-direction items: pick the term that matches the LSAT-specific
// meaning. Distractors are sibling terms from TERMS.
const REVERSE: TermQuizItem[] = [
  {
    id: "tq_reverse_1",
    prompt: "Which LSAT term means \"require or assume as a necessary condition\"?",
    options: ["Illustrate", "Presuppose", "Undermine", "Phenomenon"],
    correctOption: 1,
    explanation: "Presuppose on the LSAT simply means require. It refers to a necessary condition.",
  },
  {

Tool result:
  const distractors = METHOD_PATTERNS.filter((p) => p.id !== patternId)
    .slice((index * 3) % (METHOD_PATTERNS.length - 1), ((index * 3) % (METHOD_PATTERNS.length - 1)) + 3)
    .map((p) => p.plainEnglish);

  // Ensure 3 unique distractors. Fall back to top siblings if slice underran.
  while (distractors.length < 3) {
    const candidate = METHOD_PATTERNS.find(
      (p) => p.id !== patternId && !distractors.includes(p.plainEnglish),
    );
    if (!candidate) break;
    distractors.push(candidate.plainEnglish);
  }

  const options = [pattern.plainEnglish, ...distractors];
  // Deterministic shuffle by index so correctOption is reproducible
  const correctIndex = index % options.length;
  const reordered = [...options];
  const tmp = reordered[0];
  reordered[0] = reordered[correctIndex];
  reordered[correctIndex] = tmp;

  return {
    id: `mq_${patternId}`,
    stimulus: pattern.example.stimulus,
    prompt: "Which method of reasoning does this stimulus use?",
    options: reordered,
    correctOption: correctIndex,
    explanation: pattern.example.explanation,
  };
}

export const METHOD_QUIZ_ITEMS: MethodQuizItem[] = SELECTED_PATTERN_IDS.map((id, i) =>
  buildItem(id, i),
);

// 10 "Spot the Trap" quiz items for Module 6.
//
// Each item presents a wrong-answer choice (lifted from a real drill in
// data/drills.ts: a wrong option whose explanation describes the trap), then
// asks the student to label the trap type. The four trap-type labels come
// directly from the trapTag taxonomy already documented at the top of
// data/drills.ts. The student picks from: wrong_role, wrong_method,
// structural_mismatch, attractive_language.
//
// No new LSAT content authored: every stimulus, every wrong-answer choice,
// and every explanation is a verbatim field from data/drills.ts.

import { DRILLS } from "./drills";
import { DrillItem } from "../types";

export type TrapTag =
  | "wrong_role"
  | "wrong_method"
  | "structural_mismatch"
  | "attractive_language";

export interface TrapItem {
  id: string;
  stimulus: string;
  wrongChoice: string;
  prompt: string;
  options: TrapTag[];
  correctOption: number;
  explanation: string;
}

const TRAP_LABELS: Record<TrapTag, string> = {
  wrong_role: "Wrong Role",
  wrong_method: "Wrong Method",
  structural_mismatch: "Structural Mismatch",
  attractive_language: "Attractive Language",
};

export const TRAP_OPTION_ORDER: TrapTag[] = [
  "wrong_role",
  "wrong_method",
  "structural_mismatch",
  "attractive_language",
];

export const TRAP_OPTION_TEXT: string[] = TRAP_OPTION_ORDER.map((tag) => TRAP_LABELS[tag]);

// Pick the first 10 drills that have a defined trapTag and at least one
// wrong option with a substantive explanation. Use option[0] when it is the
// wrong answer; otherwise use the first incorrect option.
function pickWrongOption(drill: DrillItem): { text: string; explanation: string } | null {
  for (let i = 0; i < drill.options.length; i++) {
    if (i === drill.correctOptionIndex) continue;
    const opt = drill.options[i];
    if (opt.explanation && opt.explanation.length > 20) return opt;
  }
  return null;
}

const SOURCE_DRILLS = DRILLS.filter((d) => Boolean(d.trapTag)).slice(0, 10);

export const TRAP_ITEMS: TrapItem[] = SOURCE_DRILLS.map((drill, index) => {
  const wrong = pickWrongOption(drill);
  const trapTag = (drill.trapTag as TrapTag) ?? "attractive_language";
  return {
    id: `trap_${drill.id}`,
    stimulus: drill.stimulus ?? "",
    wrongChoice: wrong?.text ?? drill.options[0].text,
    prompt: "What kind of trap is this wrong answer?",
    options: TRAP_OPTION_ORDER,
    correctOption: TRAP_OPTION_ORDER.indexOf(trapTag),
    explanation: wrong?.explanation ?? drill.explanation,
  };
});
